// ---- Spawn-trigger statuses applied to the dying foe (ApplyToDead) ----
// Pattern: AMP Strange_Jewelry SJ03_05 (line 402) — `Spawn` (NOT Summon) without GROUND: prefix
//   in OnApplyFunctors. Spawn = independent NPC (not engine-level pet) → no shared vision,
//   not player-controllable. AMP_FORCE_HOSTILE as one of the applied statuses → hostile-to-all.
// Interrupt → ApplyStatus on OBSERVER_TARGET (corpse) → OnApplyFunctors fires Spawn.
// ApplyStatus is a synchronous functor (NOT a cast operation) so no race-condition with
//   the wearer's interrupted attack.
// Note: Spawn() args silently no-op for status apply on the spawned creature, so faction setup
//   happens in Osiris post-EnteredLevel:
//   T1/T2 (no PACT) → ApplyStatus AMP_FORCE_HOSTILE + Sees-loop with SetHostileAndEnterCombat.
//   T3 (PACT, no TWIN) → ApplyStatus PARTY_ALLY (FactionOverride(Source) = caster's faction).
//   T4 (PACT + TWIN) → 2× Spawn, both friendly through PARTY_ALLY.

// T1/T2: 1× spawn, hostile-to-all via AMP_FORCE_HOSTILE
new entry "MAG_NNECROTIC_SPAWN_TRIGGER_T1"
type "StatusData"
data "StatusType" "BOOST"
data "StackId" "MAG_NNECROTIC_SPAWN_TRIGGER_T1"
data "StatusPropertyFlags" "DisableOverhead;DisableCombatlog;DisablePortraitIndicator;IgnoreResting;ApplyToDead"
data "OnApplyFunctors" "Spawn(7811ecf3-2657-4820-99e8-8c6844e147e1,Projectile_AiHelper_Summon_Weak,MAG_NNECROTIC_SOUL_FLAG,AMP_FORCE_HOSTILE)"

// T3: 1× spawn, friendly (Osiris-driven via PACT_MARKER on wearer)
new entry "MAG_NNECROTIC_SPAWN_TRIGGER_T3"
type "StatusData"
data "StatusType" "BOOST"
using "MAG_NNECROTIC_SPAWN_TRIGGER_T1"
data "StackId" "MAG_NNECROTIC_SPAWN_TRIGGER_T3"
data "OnApplyFunctors" "Spawn(7811ecf3-2657-4820-99e8-8c6844e147e1,Projectile_AiHelper_Summon_Weak,MAG_NNECROTIC_SOUL_FLAG)"

// T4: 2× spawn, friendly
new entry "MAG_NNECROTIC_SPAWN_TRIGGER_T4"
type "StatusData"
data "StatusType" "BOOST"
using "MAG_NNECROTIC_SPAWN_TRIGGER_T1"
data "StackId" "MAG_NNECROTIC_SPAWN_TRIGGER_T4"
data "OnApplyFunctors" "Spawn(7811ecf3-2657-4820-99e8-8c6844e147e1,Projectile_AiHelper_Summon_Weak,MAG_NNECROTIC_SOUL_FLAG);Spawn(7811ecf3-2657-4820-99e8-8c6844e147e1,Projectile_AiHelper_Summon_Weak,MAG_NNECROTIC_SOUL_FLAG)"

// ================ ARC_R_BLOOD - BLOOD MAGIC MISSILE SYSTEM ================
// Magic Missile gains +1/2/3/4 projectiles while you are bleeding OR someone within 3m is bleeding

// ===== SHARED COMPONENTS (all tiers) =====

// Primary aura - scans for BLEEDING in 3m radius
new entry "ARC_R_BLOOD_AURA"
type "StatusData"
data "StatusType" "BOOST"
data "StackId" "ARC_R_BLOOD_AURA"
data "AuraRadius" "3"
data "AuraStatuses" "TARGET:IF(HasStatus('BLEEDING') and Character() and not Self()):ApplyStatus(ARC_R_BLOOD_PROXIMITY_MARKER)"
data "StatusPropertyFlags" "DisableOverhead;DisableCombatlog;DisablePortraitIndicator;IgnoreResting;ApplyToDead"

// Cascading reverse aura - on bleeding entities, finds owner and marks them
new entry "ARC_R_BLOOD_PROXIMITY_MARKER"
type "StatusData"
data "StatusType" "BOOST"
data "StackId" "ARC_R_BLOOD_PROXIMITY_MARKER"
data "AuraRadius" "3"
data "StackType" "Ignore"
data "AuraStatuses" "TARGET:IF(HasStatus('ARC_R_BLOOD_AURA')):ApplyStatus(ARC_R_BLOOD_TRACKER)"
data "StatusPropertyFlags" "DisableOverhead;DisableCombatlog;DisablePortraitIndicator;IgnoreResting"

// Tracker - marks owner as "someone nearby is bleeding"
